ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e2643	Please have a reply.
ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e2659	Deputies! Allow me to begin by stating that the condition of the Austrian motorways and highways is very, very bad. The following facts are responsible: The substance of the road network is old, and this leads to an increased need for renovation, and the priorities in motorway construction have certainly not been set in the past towards conservation. It was used for the maintenance of motorways only about half of what corresponds to the international average of about 1 percent. Some 2 billion schillings would have to be invested, but a maximum of half was invested in recent years. Because of the fact that the substance is really not good, I have set a focus here and provided 2 billion schilling annually for conservation, another 1.5 billion shilling for operational conservation, and in addition 120 million shilling for tunnel renovation to improve the situation.

You know, it's not that easy to intervene in existing contracts, but it's because of the good cooperation and the pressure I've exerted on the construction companies, together with the ASFINAG, that we can already work on the bad weather days on weekends and thus have a reduction of up to half a year at the large construction sites. That's a very important time profit. But very important is safety, and it starts when the construction sites are set up. It should not happen that construction sites will be set up before an extended weekend, although the actual work will only begin after the weekend. Nor should the transition areas be designed in such a way as to lead to accidents. So we have invested very much in the control, in the lighting in the entrance area, but also in the demarcation, in the central rails. These improvements have already been successful, and I am very happy about that, especially because the main journey time is now beginning and safety is very important.

Everyone will ask themselves the question: What is the cost of this good solidary health system? – For example, it costs a medium-sized employee with a gross income of 25 000 S and two children to say and write 850 S per month. Ladies and gentlemen! What is the social partnership? Since when has it been, and how has it implemented itself in the Austrian state? According to the main association of the Austrian social security funds, documented in the book "100 Years of Social Security in Austria", it is clear that on 14 April, the social security fund is in place. On July 21, 1921, with the Law on the Equality of the First Republic, the birth of social self-government in Austria must be fixed on the one hand and social partners on the other. What did this equality law bring? It has brought equality between workers' and employers' associations, the same participation in legislation, economic administration – the same powers of participation, parity in relation to 1 : 1. Ladies and gentlemen! This social partnership ended in 1938 by the invasion of the National Socialists and was in 1947 with the memorable treaty between Dr. Julius Raab and Johann Böhm were re-established. ==References== On 4 April 1946, these two, also known to today's youth as a major politician in the Second Republic, with the creation of the "Baubeiter-Urlaubskasse" on the one hand, and the agreements concerning the Austrian main association and the creation of social security institutions on the other, implemented in 1949, re-founded the social partners. Remarkable in the paper at that time is the exact distribution: 1 : 1. In the countries of Upper Austria, Salzburg and Tyrol, the chairmen are appointed by the employers, in Styria and in Carinthia by the employee side. The senior officials are to be ordered in Vienna, Styria and Carinthia from the workers' representatives of the Socialist Group, in the countries of Upper Austria, Salzburg, Tyrol and Vorarlberg from the workers' group of the People's Party Group. – This was the beginning of the social partnership in the Second Republic. On the basis of the findings of the year 1990 and of the study by my predecessor, Federal Minister Hesoun, the Housemann study in 1994 has resulted in a change in the social security system. Until then, ladies and gentlemen, it was obvious in the self-representation that there was not only a general meeting in the Austrian main association, but in addition to the general meeting there was also a committee of directors, a monitoring committee and the following section committees: Health insurance in general, Agricultural health care telephone, Farm health insurance, Accident insurance, Self-employed Pension Insurance Committee and Pension Insurance Committee ASVG. There has been what this Federal Government wants in the future: Divisions responsible! Divisional responsibility for health insurance, divisional responsibility for accident insurance, divisional responsibility for pension insurance. Ladies and gentlemen! Since the introduction of the Social Social Partnership until today, all the presidents of the main association of Austrian social security funds and their vice-presidents, respectively, in the early years, have been appointed by the respective Federal Minister for Social Affairs, but have never been elected. ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e16359	Mr President. Federal Minister! High house! Ladies and gentlemen! The reorganisation of the Home Labour Commission requires only a single amendment of § 52 of the Home Labour Act 1960 on the monitoring of remuneration. I would like to say a few words on this subject, although the Committee on Labour and Social Affairs has already reached unanimity. Ladies and gentlemen! The demand for domestic workers working in the industry has recently fallen very much. For example, at the end of June of this year only more than 17 open jobs for home workers and home workers were predetermined or reported by the economy at the end of Austria's AMS. Businesses who used to do simple work at home often now employ part-time workers with flexible working hours in the establishments and thus in better service conditions. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] This development often benefits women who are unable to do all day work for family reasons. The increased supply of part-time work with flexible working hours has recently created jobs specifically for women who now no longer have to provide part of their housing as a job and no longer have to care for work aid, equipment and energy costs. On the other hand, special low-cost work is also being carried out more often in countries where wage costs and, of course, additional labour costs are much cheaper than in Austria. Ladies and gentlemen! In view of the decline in jobs for domestic workers, the government proposes a reorganisation of the existing Home Labour Commission. It is planned to assign the pay calculators of the Home Labour Commission to the labour inspectorates and to disband the offices of the Home Labour Commission in Vienna. The new rules on the protection of remuneration in the case of under-remuneration shall also be implemented by the labour inspectorate, as well as the monitoring of compliance with the laws of the home worker's general contract and the home labour tariffs of the remuneration provisions laid down in the individual contract. In the awarding of homework, there has always been a considerable under-remuneration. If the Labour Inspectorate finds under-remuneration, it must, in accordance with the Home Labour Act, ask the client to pay the reduced amount. This is an important matter. The labour inspectorates are now responsible for ensuring that home workers are protected accordingly and come to their wages as well. [[Congratulations with the liberals and the ÖVP.]] [[Abg. Schwarzenberger: That would have been a good conclusion!]] Ladies and gentlemen! For the federal government, savings arise due to the absence of the offices of the Home Labour Commission in Vienna due to the loss of expenses for the offices as well as by saving the office of the head of the office. The reduction in the need for services of the Home Labour Commission is therefore also taken into account by this government through savings in the administration. However, the benefits for home workers remain upright. [[Unrest in the hall.]] [[President Dr. Fischer gives the bell sign.]] This is another example, ladies and gentlemen, of the fact that this government, in smaller areas of public service, is also making management cost-effective, flexible and efficient. I would therefore ask for your consent to amend the Home Labour Act 1960. Thank you.

I know what I'm talking about, I have enough interventions, almost daily, when it comes to patients need any investigations, a bed or a care place; that's really on my agenda. What has always bothered me about our health system, which we have so to speak inherited from the Socialists, is that we need interventions in order to be really optimally provided – and that the system itself is not able to provide this optimal care. Medical care is a fundamental right and must be available for every Austrian. If necessary, it must be available to it as quickly and efficiently as possible. This is our self-understanding of optimal, rapid medical care, and I am proud of that.

ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e19077	Mr President. Mr State Secretary! High house! All good things are three. This also includes the Pharmacy Chamber Act. I believe that this is another building block for our good and efficient health policy. This law establishes a modern legal basis for the legal common professional representation of employed and self-employed pharmacists. The main innovations are first of all the access of employed and independent pharmacists to all functions. Now, for example, an employee can be elected president. Secondly, the departments of the Pharmacies Chamber receive collective contractual capacity, provided that collective contracts are not, in any case, concluded by the voluntary professional associations of the pharmacists. Thirdly, there are, of course, positive effects for the population by creating quality assurance measures, including in the pharmaceutical and pharmaceutical sectors, and by introducing compulsory training for the pharmaceuticals. The Federal Government and the Health Committee have taken this government template into account the commitment of independent and employed pharmacists to a joint chamber, taking into account, in a balanced manner, the health and occupational requirements. They have thus completed a long-term reform discussion positively. This law not only achieves constitutional improvements in organisational standards, but also improvements for the employed pharmacists, since now an employee can also be elected president. I emphasize: elected and not ordered – that to the address of the SPÖ. But above all, this law will have a positive impact on the population: The Pharmacovigilance Chamber now has the competence to implement quality assurance and mandatory training measures. – The SPÖ's attitude is incomprehensible to me because, despite several explanatory talks, these do not want to understand with the individual representatives of the Pharmacies Chamber that this law is really happening for those concerned for the better. The purpose of the application is to decide on the following: ==References== In consideration of the request of the Landesgericht Linz, 27 EHv 53/01, for approval of the official persecution of the delegates to the National Council Ridi Steiblwerd within the meaning of Art. 57 para. 3 B-VG notes that there is a link between the criminal act alleged by the private prosecutor and the political activity of the delegates to the National Council of Ridi Steiblbe. 2. An official persecution of the representatives of the National Council Ridi Steiblwerd has been approved." As far as the application of the Committee on Immunities is concerned. I ask those ladies and gentlemen who follow this request for a sign of approval. – This is accepted by majority vote. Decision to terminate the ordinary meeting 2000/2001
ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e23171	As we have thus completed the agenda, I should like to make the following request from all four political groups to the House in agreement with the political groups: "The President of the Federal Republic of Germany is asked to attend the ordinary meeting of XXI 2000/2001. National Council legislative period with Friday 13th. July 2001 to be declared terminated." I ask those ladies and gentlemen who agree to this proposal to give a sign on this. This Decision was adopted unanimously. Can I ask the clubmen if there was no intention of a permanence statement? - That's not the case, good. [[Abg. Ing. Westenthaler: No!]] That concludes the vote. Inflow
ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e23209	I am still announcing that self-employed applications 494/A to 501/A have been tabled at today's meeting. Final address of the President
ParlaMint-AT_2001-07-06-021-XXI-NRSITZ-00076_d7e23232	Ladies and gentlemen! We are therefore at the end of this last summer session, and everyone will take a stand for himself. It cannot be the President's job to carry out an assessment of the very important legislative decisions we have taken. However, the fact is that a very busy half-year is behind us and that in particular a few very strenuous days and weeks are behind us. I am glad that we have managed to carry out our projects, as we decided to do at the presidential meeting in a formal manner, in the course of the meetings, and that we are doing these work at a reasonable time today. It is a real need – and I know that there is a sense with you – to say a very warm word of thanks to all the employees of the House and all the staff of the parliamentary groups at this time. You really deserved it. [[General condolences.]] It is also a need – and I would like to express this – to wish you all had a few days or weeks of rest. I wish you a nice summer. After all these efforts, everyone should spend him – with his family or with his friends – as he wishes. I'm just adding, just as I did last year, If we have now taken the decision, the meeting of the National Council by 13. To end July and start again in mid-September, that is – and I say this to the public – not two months of vacation, but then it is two months – probably – without plenary sessions, but other political work is to be done. Nevertheless: Have a nice summer! Good rest and good luck! [[General condolences.]] The sitting is closed.
